<script>




window.kim = window.kim || {};
kim.fullUserNameKey = "kim.kunde.fullUserName"
kim.fullUserNameMaxLength = 121
kim.isReloadFullName = function() {
var name = localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey]
return !name || name.length > kim.fullUserNameMaxLength
}
kim.updateUserFullName = function() {
if (localStorage[kim.showAsLoggedInKey] === "true" && kim.isReloadFullName()) {
$.get("/api/kundensvc/Kunde/Name/v1", function(data) {
if(data.length > kim.fullUserNameMaxLength) {
localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey] = ""
return
}
localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey] = data
kim.fillWelcome("Hallo " + localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey])
}).fail(function() {
})
} else if (localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey]) {
kim.fillWelcome("Hallo " + localStorage[kim.fullUserNameKey])
}
}
kim.fillWelcome = function(value) {
$("#welcomeUser").html(value)
}

window.kim = window.kim || {};
kim.showAsLoggedInKey = "kim.login.showAsLoggedIn"
kim.updateLoginLinks = function() {
var loginLinks = $(".oLogin")
var logoutLinks = $(".oLogout")
kim.onLogout(logoutLinks)
kim.processLoginState(loginLinks, logoutLinks)
}
kim.processLoginState = function(loginLinks, logoutLinks) {
try {
if (localStorage) {
if (localStorage[kim.showAsLoggedInKey] === "true") {
loginLinks.hide()
logoutLinks.show()
} else {
logoutLinks.hide()
loginLinks.show()
}
}
} catch(storageError) {}
}
kim.onLogout = function(logoutLinks) {
logoutLinks.on("click.kim", function (event) {
localStorage[kim.showAsLoggedInKey] = false
localStorage.removeItem(kim.fullUserNameKey)
kim.fillWelcome("")
kim.setzeClubstatusKeinMitglied()
document.cookie = "cclogin-data=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 UTC; path=/;";
})
}

</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
        $(document).ready(function () {
            var _href = '/filiale/headerfiliale/sap';
            var _headerFilialeId = ThaliaApp.util.getCookie('headerfiliale');

            if (_headerFilialeId === '') {
                requestHeaderFiliale(_href);
            } else {
                ThaliaApp.util.delCookie('red_lieblingsfiliale_not_available');
                $.when($.ajax(_href + "/" + _headerFilialeId))
                    .done(function (resp) {
                        var _headerfiliale = $("#headerfiliale");
                        _headerfiliale.replaceWith(resp);
                   });
            }

            if (window.trackData !== 'undefined' && typeof window.trackData.refreshTracking === 'function') {
                window.trackData.refreshTracking();
            }
        });

        function requestHeaderFiliale (href) {
            if (ThaliaApp.util.getCookie('red_lieblingsfiliale_not_available') === 'true') {
                // 
                return;
            }
            try {
                var _isLoggedIn = localStorage.getItem('kim.login.showAsLoggedIn');
            } catch(e) {
                return;
            }
            if (_isLoggedIn === 'true') {
                $.when($.ajax({url: href, headers: {'Accept': 'text/html'}}))
                    .done(function (resp) {
                        var _headerfiliale = $("#headerfiliale");
                        _headerfiliale.replaceWith(resp);
                    })
                    .fail(function (jqXHR) {
                        if(jqXHR.status === 404) {
                            ThaliaApp.util.setCookie('red_lieblingsfiliale_not_available', 'true')
                        }
                    });
            }
        }

    </script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    var geolocationOptions = {
        enableHighAccuracy: false,
        timeout: 30000,  // milliseconds (30 seconds)
        maximumAge: 600000 // milliseconds (10 minutes)
    };

    function getGeolocationForHeader() {
        var deferred = $.Deferred();
        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
            function (position) {
                deferred.resolve({
                    longitude: position.coords.longitude,
                    latitude: position.coords.latitude
                });
            },
            function (error) {
                deferred.reject(searchByGeoLocationOff());
            },
            geolocationOptions
        );
        return deferred.promise();
    }


    function searchByGeoLocation() {

        if (window.trackData !== 'undefined' && typeof window.trackData.sendinfo === 'function') {
            window.trackData.sendinfo({linkId: 'naechsteFilialeFinden'});
        }

        $.when(getGeolocationForHeader()).then(
            function (position) {
                window.location = '/shop/home/filialen/showStadt/?geo=' + position.longitude + "," + position.latitude;
            }
        );
        return false;
    }

    function searchByGeoLocationOff() {
        window.location = '/shop/home/filialen';
        return false;
    }


</script>

    (function () {
        window.kim = window.kim || {};
        var showAsLoggedInKey = kim.showAsLoggedInKey || 'kim.login.showAsLoggedIn';
        var fullUserNameKey = "kim.kunde.fullUserName";
        var fullUserNameMaxLength = 121;
        var logoutEventIdentifier = kim.logoutEventIdentifier || 'kim.logout'

        function isReloadFullName() {
            var name = localStorage[fullUserNameKey];
            return !name || name.length > fullUserNameMaxLength
        }

        function updateUserFullName() {
            if (localStorage[showAsLoggedInKey] === "true" && isReloadFullName()) {
                fetch("/api/kundensvc/Kunde/Name/v1").then(function (response) {
                    return response.text();
                }).then(function (kundenname) {
                    if (kundenname.length > fullUserNameMaxLength) {
                        localStorage[fullUserNameKey] = "";
                        return
                    }
                    localStorage[fullUserNameKey] = kundenname;
                    fillWelcome("Hallo " + localStorage[fullUserNameKey]);
                })
            }
            if (localStorage[fullUserNameKey]) {
                fillWelcome("Hallo " + localStorage[fullUserNameKey]);
            }
        }

        function fillWelcome(value) {
            document.getElementById("welcomeUser").innerText = value;
        }

        updateUserFullName();

        window.addEventListener(logoutEventIdentifier,
            function() {
                localStorage.removeItem(fullUserNameKey);
                fillWelcome("");
            })
    })();

</script>
</li>

<script language="javascript" type="text/javascript">
    kim = kim || {}
    kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey = kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey || "kim.merkzettel.artikelanzahl"
    kim.showAsLoggedInKey = kim.showAsLoggedInKey || "kim.login.showAsLoggedIn"

    kim.renderArtikelanzahl = function(artikelAnzahl) {
        var merkzettelHeaderElements = document.getElementsByTagName("merkzettel-header");
        if(merkzettelHeaderElements.length == 0){
            if (artikelAnzahl && artikelAnzahl != "0") {
                $("#merkzettel-burger-link").html("Merkzettel (" + artikelAnzahl + ")");
                $(".merkzettel-header-link").html("Merkzettel (" + artikelAnzahl + ")");
            }else{
                $("#merkzettel-burger-link").html("Merkzettel");
                $(".merkzettel-header-link").html("Merkzettel");
            }
        }else{
            for( var i = 0; i<merkzettelHeaderElements.length; i++){
                merkzettelHeaderElements[i].renderArtikelanzahl(artikelAnzahl);
            }
        }
    }

    kim.updateArtikelanzahl = function() {
        kim.withLocalStorage(function() {
            if (!(localStorage[kim.showAsLoggedInKey] === "true")) {
                kim.clearArtikelanzahl();
                kim.renderArtikelanzahl("0");
                return;
            }

            if(localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]) {
                kim.renderArtikelanzahl(localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]);
                return;
            }

            $.get("/api/rest/public/merkzettel/admin/artikelanzahl/v1", function (data) {
                        localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey] = data;
                        kim.renderArtikelanzahl(localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]);
            }).fail(function () {
                kim.clearArtikelanzahl();
            })

        })
    }

    kim.incrementArtikelanzahl = function(){
        kim.withLocalStorage(function() {
            if (localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]) {
                var neueArtikelanzahl = parseInt(localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey]) + 1;
                localStorage[kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey] = neueArtikelanzahl;
                kim.renderArtikelanzahl(neueArtikelanzahl)
            }
        })
    }

    kim.clearAndUpdateArtikelanzahl = function() {
        kim.withLocalStorage(function() {
            kim.clearArtikelanzahl();
            kim.updateArtikelanzahl();
        })
    }

    kim.clearArtikelanzahl = function(){
        kim.withLocalStorage(function() {
            localStorage.removeItem(kim.merkzettelArtikelanzahlKey);
        })
    }

    kim.withLocalStorage = function(callback) {
        try {
            if (localStorage) {
                callback();
            }
        } catch (storageError) {}
    }
</script>

<script>
        fetch(
            '/api/warenkorb/size',
            {
                method: 'GET',
                cache: 'no-store',
                headers:
                    {
                        'Accept': 'application/json'
                    },
                credentials: 'include'
            }
        )
            .then(function (response) {
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('Request: ' + response.url + ' Status Code: ' + response.status);
                }

                return response.json();
            })
            .then(function (responseJson) {
                if (responseJson.anzahlArtikel < 1) {
                    return;
                }
                document.getElementById('artikelAnzahlWarenkorb').innerHTML = responseJson.anzahlArtikel;
            })
            .catch(function (err) {
                var trackData = window.trackData;
                if(trackData) {
                    trackData.sendinfo({}, 'WarenkorbsymbolFetchError', 'click');
                }
            })
    </script>

<script language="javascript" type="text/javascript">
    // Inputfeld mit Loginname vorausfüllen, wenn vorhanden
    $(function () {
        var $newsletterInput = $('#input-NewsletterAnmelden');
        if (localStorage && $newsletterInput.val() === "") {
            $newsletterInput.val(localStorage["kim.login.loginUsername"])
        }
    })

    // Newsletter-Link unter Mein Konto
    $('a[href="#ocsNewsletterContainer"]').click(function(event) {
        // Klick auf den Link tracken
        window.trackData.sendinfo({}, "Mein_Konto_Newsletter_bestellen", "click")

        // Inputfeld fokussieren, nachdem Newsletter-Link geklickt wurde
        $('input#input-NewsletterAnmelden').focus()
        event.preventDefault()
    })

    // Inputfeld fokussieren, wenn beim Laden der Newsletter-Anker in der Location ist (z.B. Newsletter aus CH-Mail)
    if (location.hash === "#ocsNewsletterContainer") {
        $('input#input-NewsletterAnmelden').focus()
    }
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    var $callout = $("#callout-generalMessageNewsletter");

    if($callout && $callout.is(":visible")) {
        // Erfolg der Anmeldung tracken
        if ($callout.hasClass("success")) {
            window.trackData.sendinfo({linkId: "Newsletter_bestellen_Erfolg"});
        }

        // Fehler bei Anmeldung tracken
        if ($callout.hasClass("alert")) {
            window.trackData.add({
                "customParameter": {
                    15: ""   // Fehler bei Newsletter-Anmeldung
                }
            }, {merge: true})
        }
    }

    // Button-Klick tracken
    $("button[data-test='subscribeNewsletter-button']").click(function (event) {
        window.trackData.sendinfo({}, "Newsletter_bestellen", "click");
    });
</script>
